## 2026-07-13 — LONG runner BE-lock SHIPPED (`runner_trail_be_ratchet_enabled` false→true, lock +0.10)

**Decision.** Re-enable the LONG armed-runner BE ratchet (operator-disabled Jun 25): once a LONG runner arms (peak ≥ 0.45%), the exit floor clamps to `max(peak − 1.0×ATR, +0.10)`. Config flip only — engine (realtime tick path since 07-06), UI toggle, and load/save were already wired; `runner_trail_be_lock_pct` stays 0.10. Operator words: "Activate the BE 0.10 for LONGs, ship it, commit and push."

**Evidence.** ① Pre-committed gate (registered 07-06) fired: 8/8 fresh armed longs, cumulative +0.70pp, zero negatives (PUMP +0.13, USELESS +0.57, AAVE + 5 ties). ② Pooled, gate-grade: N=38 armed ML runners, lock +6.11pp vs actual, 14 better/9 worse, 10/3 dates. ③ Same-session full-baseline head-to-head (this ship's trigger): atr10+BE +18.35% vs lockless atr10 +17.62% (+0.73pp floor benefit) vs actual +15.17%. ④ atr05 (N 1.0→0.5) refuted a THIRD time and now strictly dominated: atr05+BE +12.41% (−5.94pp vs atr10+BE) — the floor rescues sagging winners (atr05's only benefit) without the runner tax (ACT −2.83pp, NEAR −1.45, WLD −1.31 under the tight trail). Do not re-propose tighter N; the floor is the correct instrument.

**Tracking (roles invert at ship).** Live = locked; the existing lockless `atr10` shadow column becomes the revert monitor (rides Order columns → CSV/pool for free). Per batch: Δ = actual − atr10-raw shadow, cumulative. Floor fires observable as RUNNER_TRAIL closes at ~+0.10 (`[REALTIME_RUNNER_TRAIL]` floor log); post-exit regret table gives trap color (PostPeak% after a floor fire) but the shadow prices it. 🔒 REVERT GATE (locked at ship): revert to false if cumulative Δ < 0 on N≥10 fresh armed LONGs across ≥3 dates. Method note: baseline head-to-head approximates the floor as max(shadow, +0.10) on armed trades — gap-through slippage applies equally to both sides, ranking robust.

Scope: momentum-ladder filters only (engine-level gates show 0 — first-fail only).

Effect at next 6h check: reserve tops up $11→$50.

## 2026-07-14 — Funnel v2: PAIR_RSI_ADX_CROSS sub-rule breakdown + Sole share (L%/S%) columns (operator-requested)

**① Sub-rule breakdown ("open the filter by the real blocker").** The top Sole filter is a COMPOSITE (`30-35:25,35-50:30`) — lumped counts couldn't say which RSI band carries the cost. New `_rsi_adx_block_rule()` in indicators.py returns the exact matching rule string (boolean `_passes_rsi_adx_filter` is now a wrapper — decision byte-identical, equivalence-tested); both ladder branches record `PAIR_RSI_ADX_CROSS[<rule>]` into the v2 stores while the LEGACY counter gets the stripped parent name (Total continuity). Summary aggregates variants under the parent row (Sole RANKING unaffected — composite competes as one filter) + splices `↳ [rule]` child rows beneath (v2 columns only, legacy zeros, excluded from footer totals). Persistence handles suffixed keys as-is. Generalizes: any future composite can adopt the `NAME[variant]` convention for free. ## 2026-07-14 — Last-mile gate counters: PAIR_EMA_GAP_5_20 / EMA5_STRETCH / PAIR_ADX_CONFIDENCE (deep-review finding F1)

**Trigger.** Operator: GAP_MIN funnel traffic heavy yet ~no GAPMIN probe trades — "deep dive, find the bug." Deep review (agent-verified, code-traced) found the structural cause: after the evaluate-all ladder passes clean, candidates still face `check_gap_and_mode()` — the EMA5-20 gap min/max (live L min 0.05), the EMA5 stretch min/max, the confidence trade-mode, and the ADX≥adx_strong confidence floor — and every one of those rejections recorded NOTHING (no counter, no fail, no log). GAPMIN-band candidates die there by construction: small EMA5-8 gap ⇒ EMA5-20 distance near zero ⇒ killed by the 0.05 min invisibly. The probe was structurally unable to measure "young trends" while an un-waived correlated gate rejected exactly that profile. Also refuted en route: the probe slot guard as a suppressor (operator: book sits at 0-1 open, guard ≤3 never binds); ladder/helper mismatch (byte-equivalent, no untagged full-size leak in steady state — one low-probability mid-scan config-change race noted, not fixed); flips (alive, dormant by design in HEALTHY_BULL; Funnel-v2 restructure did NOT sever the trigger — engine byte-identical, counters prove mid-stack execution daily).

**Ship (instrumentation only, decision byte-identical).** `check_gap_and_mode_reason()` returns None or the exact fail tag (`PAIR_EMA_GAP_5_20[<min]/[>max]`, `EMA5_STRETCH[<min]/[>max]`, `CONF_MODE`); the boolean `check_gap_and_mode` is now a wrapper (legacy EXTREME/HIGH call sites unchanged). Both momentum emission blocks record the last-mile rejection into legacy + Funnel v2 (`PAIR_ADX_CONFIDENCE` when ADX ≤ adx_strong). These fails are sole-by-definition (candidate passed everything else) — the Sole column now sees the true last-mile cost. Smoke-tested: ladder-clean + gap520<min → records `PAIR_EMA_GAP_5_20[<min]` sole; ADX 14 → `PAIR_ADX_CONFIDENCE`; clean pass → LONG signal, zero records.

**🔒 READ GATE.** After ~2 days: if PAIR_EMA_GAP_5_20[<min] soles ≫ GAPMIN probe fires, the honest GAPMIN experiment requires deciding whether probes should waive/scale the 5-20 min inside the band (tagged, one change) — else the N≥30 verdict describes only candidates that cleared an unrelated gate. Decision deferred until the count exists.

VERY_STRONG longs + the first shorts in weeks: LIT, XLM) and killed at the ENGINE layer by BTC_SLOPE_GATE — the BTC 5m EMA20 slope dead-band (`macro_trend_flat_threshold_long=0.02 / short=0.03`): no LONG unless slope ≥ +0.02, no SHORT unless ≤ −0.03. **Ship (probe #3, operator-directed same night).** `slopegate_probe_enabled=true` + `slopegate_probe_max_open=3`: Phase-1 gate falls through (tagged, candidate must still pass EVERY other engine gate — cohort = sole-blocked by the dead-band); survivors open 1x SLOPEGATE_PROBE (shares gap-probe 0.5x/0.05x sizing; last-2-slots guard; cap rejection records BTC_SLOPE_GATE). Legacy BTC_SLOPE_GATE counter freezes while probing (GAPFLAT convention). D11 complete (config+JSON+UI toggle/max-open, 3 id hits, load/save, config-report line); D12: SLOPEGATE (probe) · LONG/SHORT rows in the 🪟 probe table (server-side rows → UI + both exports); screen_pool drops the tag.

**🔒 GATES (pre-committed, PER SIDE):** N≥30 (≥5 dates — flow ~40/day): WR≥60% & avg≥+0.15% → dead-band relaxation discussion (lower/zero the flat thresholds with evidence); WR≤45% or avg<0 → gate VINDICATED (its first evidence-grade validation since April), probe off. At verdict: slice by |BTC slope| sub-band + regime; watch DOA% (the April theory predicts DOA chop deaths). ⚠ Throughput note: at ~40 candidates/day and max 3 concurrent 1x probes, worst-case drag ≈ a few $ per losing probe — bounded by design.

Persistence handles the 3-part key natively. ② Funnel v2: suppression REMOVED entirely (a suppressed sole block is a real lost trade — the regime rule was never an actual trade veto); v2 counts every direction in every regime from Jul 15. ③ Zero-counter holes closed with counters + logs: PAIR_HELD (one-position-per-pair skips — highest-frequency invisible rejection), COOLDOWN (5-min re-entry suppression), NO_BALANCE (investment≤0 — balance starvation previously indistinguishable from strict filters). Explainers updated on all 3 surfaces.

## 2026-07-16 — Macro separator deep sweep (operator-driven, PROBE VERDICT PROTOCOL applied to the live batch) → 2 watches, no ship, GAPFLAT stays probe-sized
**Question.** Operator hypothesis: expanding/non-expanding is not a real filter — a macro (BTC) condition separates losers. ## 2026-07-17 — Wiring audit RESOLVED: BTC RSI window is dead code; the 5m dead-band is the de facto RSI floor
Trace: `btc_rsi_min_long=40 / btc_rsi_max_long=65` are set in live config but enforced ONLY inside the `btc_global_enabled` block (services/trading_engine.py ~8159; May-5 comment: "BTC RSI min/max stays GATED by btc_global_enabled per user direction") — and `btc_global_filter_enabled=false`. The window runs for NOBODY. The config report prints "BTC RSI L: 40-65" one line below "BTC Global: false" — inert values, easy misread (it fooled the Jul-17 morning analysis into a "purity bug" hypothesis).
Why zero sub-40 normal longs across 100+ trades/both eras anyway: sub-40 BTC RSI ⇒ BTC falling ⇒ 5m slope < flat threshold ⇒ every normal long candidate dies at BTC_SLOPE_GATE before any RSI check would matter. **The dead-band and the RSI floor are entangled: part of the gate's measured value IS capitulation-blocking**, invisible until the SLOPEGATE probe opened the door (9 sub-40 fills · 33% · −0.59% = first-ever measurement of that function; RSI-clean remainder 12·50%·−0.21 = the gate's collateral damage on basing/recovery tape).
Consequences: ① no probe bug — "sole-blocked" literally true for all 21; cohort integrity stands; ② SLOPEGATE verdict decomposes the gate into markdown-blocking (working) vs basing-blocking (costly); ③ 🔒 any half-open MUST pair with a standalone explicit BTC RSI floor (nothing else enforces one; do NOT enable btc_global for this — it bundles regime/pair-regime blocks); ④ housekeeping: UI BTC RSI fields silently inert — D11 decoupling candidate if the window is ever wanted independently.

### 1. Flip-short starvation audit → Funnel-v2 flip-chain instrumentation SHIPPED (`6551e88`)
Trigger: operator — zero flip-shorts for >1 week, "99% there MUST be a bug; check the last filter we added" (TRENDGAP, Jul-8).
**Audit: NO bug.** `_flip_filters` TRENDGAP block verified line-by-line (fail-open; sentinel `_tgmin<0` activates; blocks `btc_trend_gap ≤ −0.22`); config `flip_short_btc_trend_gap_min=-0.22` correct; PASS-phantom seeding wired. Last flip ever = SUIUSDT 2026-07-08 07:41 — the exact day BOTH the trendgap gate shipped AND B1H was re-hardened (`flip_short_btc_1h_slope_admit_mult` 1.0→0.0).
**Cause = conjunctive ~12-gate shutdown, dominated by macro gates that are ~always true in a grinding bull.** Counters since the v2 window: BTC30_RISE 1,431 (`flip_short_btc30_rise_block_min=0.0` = block ANY 30m-RSI rise) · REGIME 569 (HEALTHY_BULL) · B1H 474 (slope>0, hard) · TRENDGAP 195 · RSI_MIN 164 · QUALITY 103 · BEAR_MIN 3-4 (candidates die upstream). Net-admissibility: reverting trendgap alone restores ~nothing — its blocked flow dies downstream anyway.
**Operator Q&A:** are the 31 baseline flips (81%·+$589 live / +$692@1×) eligible today? YES — SCREENED_BASELINE v14 (frozen Jul-10) runs the real `_flip_filters`, and no flip filter changed since. So starvation = "market conditions rather than filters" is a false dichotomy: the filters ARE regime gates and the regime they require (falling/exhausted BTC — the Jun-17→Jul-8 bear/chop where the 81% WR was earned) hasn't occurred since Jul-8. Candidates ARE generated constantly (the counters prove it); the gates veto them. The Jul-6→8 B1H admit experiment already tested opening one gate in this tape and lost. Dormancy is the sleeve working as designed.
**Ship (operator: "build it, commit and push"):** `_flip_filters` refactored first-fail-wins → EVALUATE-ALL, collecting the full fail list; returns 6-tuple (…, fails) with blocked verdict, reason=fails[0], size/lev/exit byte-identical (legacy counters / [FLIP_FILTER] logs / PASS-phantom seeds untouched). Call site feeds `_record_filter_multi(fails, dir, pair)` — same Sole/Episodes/AllF stores as the momentum ladder, so FLIP_* rows render on every surface automatically (D12 satisfied with zero surface changes; D11 n/a — no config). Verified: py_compile + 5-case smoke battery (multi-fail order=code order; first-fail reason preserved incl. BTC30_RISE as expected #1; sole isolation; clean admit w/ strpk exit; PAIR_RSI_OB early-return namespace intact; FLIP_LONG_DISABLED supersede order). Deploy green (Source+Deploy Succeeded).
**Read rule (locked in CURRENT_STATE):** after 2-3 days — one gate with meaningful FLIP Sole = the marginal blocker → loosen-discussion armed with its PASS-phantom WR; fails arriving as macro bundles with Sole≈0 = confirmed regime dormancy, NO filter changes on starvation alone.

## 2026-07-18 — SLOPEGATE verdict-day deep pass (by-slope slice, full 43-var W/L table, 2D sweep) + quiet-pair-tape finding (multiplier-target; de-mux refuted)

### 0. Gate mechanics recap (operator asked for the plain statement)
BTC_SLOPE_GATE = the BTC 5m EMA20-slope dead-band ("Min BTC EMA20 Slope", Apr-14, N=4 calibration). LONG candidates are blocked unless slope ≥ +0.02 (flat OR falling BTC = blocked); SHORT mirror at ≤ −0.03. SLOPEGATE_PROBE = candidates that passed EVERY other gate and died only here, opened real at ~1× — the probe cohort IS the blocked zone, measuring "what would we have made without the gate."

### 1. By-slope slice (the pre-registered verdict question) — N=25 (11W/14L, 4 dates), 1×
<−0.10: 3·33%·−$226 | −0.10..−0.05: 5·20%·−$455 | −0.05..−0.04: 5·80%·+$134 | −0.04..−0.03: 4·50%·−$162 | −0.03..−0.02: 2·50%·−$89 | −0.01..0: 3·33%·−$186 | 0..+0.01: 2·0%·−$172 | +0.01..+0.02: 1·100%·+$26.
Grouped: DEEP <−0.05 = 8·25%·−0.693%·−$681 · MODERATE −0.05..−0.02 = 11·64%·−0.086%·−$116 · NEAR-FLAT −0.02..+0.02 = 6·33%·−0.451%·−$332.
ANSWER to the pre-registered "flat-band vs counter-slope": INVERTED — the flat-band is NOT safe (2 of 3 fresh adds lost there: WLD −0.71, KITE −0.97); the only livable band is moderate-counter and it's breakeven. The −0.05..−0.04 fine-bin spike (80%) is sandwiched between losing bins = non-monotonic small-N noise per rules. Mechanism (3rd lens, consistent with BTC-RSI<33 knife-catch + BTC-hourly cluster): longs fired while BTC is mid-drop die; the same signal after 5m deceleration pays ~nothing but doesn't die.

### 2. Full winner-vs-loser table — ALL 43 entry variables (operator: "you didn't check ADX/confidence")

## 2026-07-22 (later) — NONEXP·SBULL implementation spec + probe-closure amendment (docs only)

Follow-up design discussion on the pre-registered NONEXP·SBULL gate. Three decisions locked into the gate's implementation spec (CURRENT_STATE):

1. **One toggle, not two.** The promotion executes on the POOLED cohort (GAPFLAT + GMINFLAT classes in STRONG_BULL) — the gate and revert gate are defined on the pool, so the switch must control the pool atomically (`nonexp_sbull_enabled` + `nonexp_sbull_mult`, own cell tag NONEXP_SBULL, one cohort row). Two toggles would allow a half-on cohort that no longer matches the revert gate's definition. Class split (flat vs flat+small) stays reconstructible from entry_gap vs the gap-min threshold. GAPMIN proper (small-but-EXPANDING) explicitly excluded — different class, never in the pooled evidence.

2. **Dedicated sizing cell, bypassing UNMATCHED.** Jul-22 analysis showed the UNMATCHED 2×/PVR≥0.9-de-mux stack is adverse for this flow (best 6/10 de-muxed; only 2 would see 2×, flipping the sample −$91). The promoted cell carries its own multiplier per the pass branch.

3. **Probe closure on promotion (operator-directed).** Operator: once promoted, close the probes and stop tracking — agreed, formalized as part of the verdict: promotion = terminal verdict for GAPFLAT + GMINFLAT (toggles off everywhere, N frozen, rows ✗ verdict-executed), logged two-sided ("pays in S.BULL / vindicated elsewhere" — the non-S.BULL pooled side reads 14·50%·−0.155% today; if ambiguous at read time, log "vindicated at thresholds, no reopen trigger" instead). Revert path reinstates the probes so the flow never goes fully dark. Side benefit: kills the probe-starvation / split-N-clock bookkeeping problem outright.

No code shipped; spec activates only if/when the pre-registered gate passes.

## 2026-07-22 (night) — TIGHT-TRAIL KILL-ZONE BUG FIX shipped (operator-identified)

Operator flagged the MIRA close (TRAILING_STOP L1 +0.449% → +19.1% post-exit in 30min) and asserted TRAILING_STOP should not exist — only the runner trail. Flips retain armed-only suppression (their strpk exit design assumed it; flip-LONGs deferred per Jun-24). Backstops (EMA13-strict, hard SL, HARD_TP, NO_EXPANSION) untouched. Blast radius small & favorable: 2/131 batch closes; MIRA-type (+$66 under HARD_TP cap) vs LDO-type (−$1 probe-sized) in the only two observed cases; the 33 Positive-No-BE SL trades were never tight-trail-saved. py_compile clean. Revert gate locked in CURRENT_STATE (sub-arm reversal cost ≥ −$150 norm on N≥10 / ≥3 dates → restore armed-only).

If confirmed at verdict, ship = "admit deadband-band entries only when pADX>23" (condition INSIDE the deadband check — slightly more than the registered pure threshold move; verdict weighs it), NOT a global filter. ③ Breadth 63.6: baseline both positive (100%·+0.60 vs 82%·+0.29) — mild gradient everywhere, kill-shot only for late-momentum RSIADX entries (86%·+0.36 vs 17%·−0.55).

**Methodological rule adopted: probe separators are class-conditional until proven otherwise — ALWAYS run the baseline main-effect test before generalizing a cohort finding.** The baseline passed the full stack, so both sides of every split are profitable there; what probes discover is WHY each blocked class fails (no-impulse entries need calm BTC · no-tailwind entries need pair trend · late-momentum entries need an uncrowded market) — each defect class has its own missing ingredient, which is why conditions live in the gates, not the filter stack.

Zero WS ticks since entry; realtime SL/exit machinery price-starved (genuine live-risk class). Scanner unaffected (REST-based), so new trades kept opening while open ones sat blind. Preceded by the 00:17 UTC app-deploy restart (docs push e908443).
**Root cause (code-read confirmed):** subscribe-during-connect RACE in `websocket_tracker._run_forever` — the stream URL is built from `subscribed_pairs` BEFORE connecting; a pair subscribed during the connect window skips `_reconnect()` (`self.websocket` still None) and is then PERMANENTLY absent from the live connection's stream list. Signature matches exactly: healthy connection, other pairs ticking, the two post-restart entries silent forever.
**Compounding recovery holes:** ① `_staleness_reconnect` SKIPPED reconnect whenever open orders existed ("protect position tracking") — inverted logic, since reconnects preserve tracking data by the module's own design (only force_reset clears); the guard made a dead stream unrecoverable exactly when positions were open. ② Staleness detection only counted received zero-price messages — total silence was invisible; no per-pair or per-connection silence watchdog existed.
**Live remediation:** `aws elasticbeanstalk restart-app-server` (sanctioned remedy, never rebuild) at ~01:3x UTC — operator confirmed prices moving again.
**Fix shipped (4 parts):** ① stale-stream reconnect UNCONDITIONAL (open-orders callback kept for log context only) + new public `force_reconnect(reason)` + `pair_silence_seconds(pair)`. ② Race closed: `_run_forever` snapshots the pair set at URL build; post-connect mismatch → immediate reconnect with the full list. ③ Connection-level watchdog: `recv` under `asyncio.wait_for(SILENCE_TIMEOUT=60)` — 60s of total silence on a multi-stream connection = dead → reconnect (catches hung-but-open sockets that pings miss). ④ Per-open-order watchdog in `update_open_positions`: tracker silent >90s (or never ticked) → REST `get_current_price` fallback feeds the price into the tracker (self-limited to ~1 call/90s/pair because the update refreshes the silence clock; ban-aware helper) + forced WS reconnect rate-limited globally to 1/120s. `update_price` now creates a missing tracker so the fallback clock works. Old "no REST fallback to avoid bans" comment replaced — the fallback is bounded and only fires for OPEN orders on a silent stream.
**Property after fix:** an open position can be price-blind for at most ~90s under ANY stream failure mode (missing stream, dead connection, silent connection). Smoke-tested (silence clock, tracker auto-create, unconditional staleness reconnect, force_reconnect without socket). Relation to GO-LIVE ②: the exchange-side resting stop still owns the bot-DEAD tail; this closes the bot-alive-stream-dead tail.

## 2026-07-27 PM — WS frozen-price ROUND 2 (HBAR): why only spike-class pairs freeze + why the new watchdog never fired (SHIPPED)
**Incident:** HBARUSDT (12:39:29 UTC entry) frozen at entry for 22+ min — LESS than a day after the 4-part hardening. Operator observation "only happens with spike chase trades" was the decisive clue. Live remediation: second sanctioned app-server restart (~13:0x UTC).
**Root cause A — the ordering hole (explains the spike-only pattern, predates yesterday's fix):** `open_position` calls `force_reset_tracking(pair, price)` FIRST — which ADDS the pair to `subscribed_pairs` — then `subscribe_pair(pair)`, whose reconnect condition was `if pair not in subscribed_pairs`. The pair is always already present → the reconnect NEVER fires for a genuinely-new pair. Top-50 pairs don't care (already in the live stream via batch subscribe); spike-scanner discoveries live OUTSIDE the top-50 stream, and order-open is their ONLY subscription path → they are deterministically, permanently absent from the connection. EVAA/FLOCK (Jul-27 AM) and HBAR (momentum trade on a pair that had rotated out of the streamed set) all match. Yesterday's subscribe-during-connect race fix could not catch this: no connect attempt ever happens.
**Root cause B — the watchdog was blinded by a pre-existing echo:** `update_open_positions` calls `websocket_tracker.update_price(pair, current_price)` EVERY monitor cycle, feeding the tracker's own stale price back into `tracker.update()` — which refreshed `last_update` — which is what `pair_silence_seconds` read. Silence never exceeded ~1s; the 90s watchdog could mathematically never fire. Yesterday's smoke test exercised the tracker in isolation, not with the monitor echo — SAME LESSON as the quiet-boost hard-cap no-op: smoke tests must exercise the FULL chain.
**Fix shipped:** ① Dual-clock: `PriceTracker.last_tick` stamped ONLY by real WS messages (`_handle_message` → `update(price, tick=True)`) and by the REST watchdog fallback (`update_price(..., tick=True)` — keeps the ~1/90s/pair self-limit); the monitor echo stays `tick=False`; `pair_silence_seconds` reads `last_tick`. Entry/reset price stamps the clock too (grace period on open). ② New `self._connected_pairs` = the pair set the LIVE connection's URL was built from (stamped at connect); `subscribe_pair` and `subscribe_pairs_batch` now reconnect whenever a requested pair is missing from `_connected_pairs`, regardless of `subscribed_pairs` membership — closing the force_reset-first ordering and healing any subscribed-but-unstreamed pair on the next batch cycle. **Verification:** 5 full-chain smoke tests incl. 50 echo cycles against a 120s-silent tracker (clock survives), and the exact engine call order `force_reset_tracking → subscribe_pair` (reconnect now fires). **Defense-in-depth stack after this:** stream-add on subscribe (fix A) → batch-cycle healing → 60s connection watchdog → 90s per-position REST fallback + forced reconnect (now actually able to fire).
**Post-review amendment (same day):** code review confirmed both bugs closed, no criticals; its IMPORTANT catch applied — a quiet-but-HEALTHY thin pair (exactly the spike cohort: legitimate >90s gaps between trades) would have force-reconnected the whole stream every 120s for the position's life, blipping ALL positions ~1-2s blind each time for zero benefit. Forced reconnect now gated on new public `is_pair_streamed(pair)` (pair actually missing from the live connection — the only case a reconnect helps); REST price fallback stays unconditional. Also fixes the startup one-shot spurious reconnect. Stale last_update comment corrected.

## 2026-07-28 — ⏱ BE-LOCK SHADOW Phase 1 shipped (observation-only) + Fast-Exit CF verdict
**Trigger:** operator asked about the Fast-Exit CF (this batch showed +$96–233 on the long side). Verdict: NO-SHIP — regime-fitted (measured on the −$145 bleed fortnight where any early profit-grab wins), 8% coverage, caps the runner winners that ARE the book's edge (locked rule: caps for losers, never on the whole book), short side N=1 (STORJ), and the baseline CF is uncomputable (pool CSVs carry no intra-trade snapshots — directionally it would flip negative in the winner-rich baseline). BE was deliberately disabled May 22-23 for the same reason.

**The honest residue:** losers-saved re-confirms the "Positive, No BE" bleed anatomy (52 trades −$664: peak +0.2/0.4 early → roll to −0.76). Right-shaped tool = time-boxed BE-LOCK (arm only if peak ≥X by minute Y — floors risk without capping upside), NOT a take-profit.

**Phase 1 build (this ship):** ① 6 Order columns `belock_t{15,20,30}_min` / `belock_tr{15,20,30}` = first-touch minute of +X% + post-touch trough, tracked in the realtime 1s tick loop (momentum book only, spikes excluded), stamped at close via the cache-sync block; restart-taint guard (cache reseeds with peak ≥0.15 → all NULL, sequencing never fabricated). ② `_compute_be_lock_shadow` 27-cell CF grid X{0.15,0.20,0.30}×Y{5,10,15}×L{+0.05,0,−0.15}: per cell fired/locks/Saved$/Killed$(full-freight winner cost)/netΔ/dates + gate-aware verdict strings. ③ ⏱ UI table + BOTH exports (D12; columns ride the CSV free). No config fields — nothing to dial, pure shadow. **🔒 Phase-2 gate locked BEFORE data: ship best cell only at N≥30 fires · ≥5 dates · net>0 post-haircut(30–50%) · Killed$ ≤ ½ Saved$. Phase 3 = 3 config fields default-OFF, only if gate clears.** Adverse prior on record: winners' avg trough −0.35 → true-BE (L=0) expected to fail; −0.15 half-floor is where the trade-off likely lives. Verified: py_compile ×4, node --check ×4, synthetic-trade CF smoke test (saved/killed/window edges exact). Zero trading-logic impact.

## 2026-07-30 PM — Spike species: maker entry bypassed, direct taker (operator-caught execution leak)
Operator asked whether spikes attempt the 20s maker entry. Audit: YES — and 18/18 lifetime spike fires (10 fades + 8 chases) closed as TAKER_FALLBACK with ZERO maker fills. Structural: the maker limit posts at the passive side of the book while a spike by definition runs away from it; the order fills only if the move stalls instantly (= the trigger was wrong). Cost: every fire paid up to the full 20s timeout in adverse drift (paper fallback re-prices at the CURRENT price after the wait) and captured $0 fee savings. Materiality on this species: ERA's entire trade lasted 19s (−1.2%); fades sell a decaying top (20s late = systematically lower fill on a short); chases enter more extended (toward/past the new 1.5×ATR guard). Fix: open_position forces taker for spike_chase/spike_fade (both live + paper paths via the shared maker_enabled gate), log [SPIKE_TAKER]; fee delta +0.027%/entry vs a ≥0.5%/5min trigger candle. Momentum/flip maker flow untouched (87 maker fills · +$80 saved book-wide — the slow-signal case where maker earns its keep). Read note registered in #11b: post-07-30 spike fills price ~20s earlier — cross-boundary entry-quality comparisons must expect a favorable level shift.

## 2026-07-30 PM — MAJORS probe hotfix (review-caught critical containment leak, d19654f)
Post-push code review of 65029a9 found the ship's core mechanism (keeping a no-trade major's signal alive through the ladder) reopened the FAN flip trigger for BTC/ETH at full flip sizing: the fan gate's block branch calls _maybe_open_flip (no no-trade check anywhere on that path), flips carry no probe tag so the MAJORS cap block's 'not flip_source' condition waves them through, and FAN_RATIO_GATE is registered at 1.0 size. Exposure ~1h, paper mode, never exercised (zero majors candidates reached the fan gate — verified no FLIP:* rows on BTC/ETH). Fix (three layers): no-trade guards at the top of _maybe_open_flip (covers FAN + PAIR_RSI_OB + LONG_UNMATCHED call sites) and _maybe_open_bull_long (latent), plus a structural open_position invariant — a no_trade_pairs order may open ONLY as MAJORS_PROBE, logged loudly on violation — so containment is enforced at the single choke-point rather than per call site. Also from the same review: MAJORS cap-rejection now maintains _last_pair_block_reason; Cell/Door dropdown options built via createElement/textContent (open-ended tag values never interpolated into markup). Lesson recorded: a fall-through probe that un-blocks an EARLY gate re-exposes every LATER branch that assumed the gate's population never reached it — future fall-through ships must sweep downstream branch-offs (flip seeds, sleeves, scanners) at design time, not review time.

REGISTERED: mandatory ≥$10M slice at the fade N≥25-30 read; pre-committed trigger — liquid cohort ≤45% WR ∨ Σ<0 at its own N≥10 while the remainder is healthy → discussion of a fade-specific ABSOLUTE notional cap (caps-for-the-losing-cohort; never a volume entry filter). Standing read rule adopted: judge the fade sleeve on Avg P&L%, never raw $, for exactly this concentration reason.
